# Review — 000-slash-commands

## Summary

Five passes over 000's window since its reopen (diff-base 734b427c, base-source reopen) to 4d681fad: 000's own commits (78a6ff34, f84975a0, 878c1a7b, fa3a4c83, f0874a16, 32eb5abf, b255cd1f, f6cdf7f2, 116307bf, 44f86f91); b1d319f1 (chore: drop Auggie, Antigravity and OpenCode support), which names no spec; 63d35a1d, d6d0affd and a70ff74c, which name none and touch the constitution and 000's files; 4d681fad, which names none and touches AGENTS.md; and this run's uncommitted fixes. Six agents (R1, R1b, R2, R3, R4 over the 43 entries in scope when the passes began, R5 over the eight that joined after) each read the nine loaded rule files whole.

Coverage: examined counts 49 of the 51 scope entries, each read whole. unread names two. runtime/src/schema/primitives.rs is 459 KB, past what one agent reads beside the rules: b1d319f1's hunks and every item they touch were read whole. scripts/mutation/destination-checks.py changed only by this run's fixes: its diff hunks and docstring were read, and every re-anchored mutant was checked against host_config.rs.

Fixed in the run: the QUAL-GROUND-001 SHOULD. config_dirs_name_every_registered_agent_and_no_other holds CONFIG_DIRS to the agent registry's config directories and apart from OTHER_TOOL_DIRS, and mutants M183 and M184 show it failing when either drifts. Fifteen observations are fixed too, two of them broken contracts of target.md:

- its in-progress next action called read-tasks without saying that an absent tasks.md makes it error;
- it lacked the §spec-lifecycle done-stop pointer that the a-done-stop scenario and 000's plan describe.

The other thirteen fixes are chores, the last from fix round 1's re-read NOTE:

- HostRow::CommandOrSkill is renamed Command;
- two tests' .agents fixtures become .pi;
- a doc comment fused onto the wrong test is moved back;
- the exec fixtures name .ductus/session.toml;
- target.md invokes read-tasks, lists the criterion back-edge, and says --clear on an absent session file writes an empty one;
- amend.md's write list and description name the criterion route;
- 000's plan and two scenarios are corrected;
- the a-done-stop scenario's Resolved Questions say /target's row still points at /amend as 023 AC30 requires, as its Behavior does.

The command copies and help tables are regenerated from the changed sources.

Deferred: QUAL-CLAIM-001 (SHOULD, high confidence, which a fixture reproduced) goes to 067's task 31, by the owner on 2026-11-14. check-artifacts is 067's primitive.

Low confidence: BE-PAYLOAD-001 on dashboard's inline config lists and scenario context-summary. They grow with one operator-written file, not the corpus. 022's task 32 bounds the unknown-keys list.

Eight observations are waived by the rule, each with its reason.

process-waivers and process-decisions applied nothing: 000 stored no waivers or decisions.

2 fix round(s): 2 re-read, 0 diff-checked, 0 exempt, 0 not re-read.

## SHOULD violations (advisory)

## Low-confidence findings

### LOW-CONFIDENCE: BE-PAYLOAD-001 — dashboard's file form leaves config.disabled-rule-files, config.unknown-keys and the targeted scenario's context-summary inline and unbounded

- **File**: `runtime/src/primitives/dashboard.rs:75-113`
- **Rule**: Response payloads MUST have a bounded maximum size. Endpoints that return collections able to exceed a small fixed maximum MUST paginate per `BE-PAGE` (api-backend.md) rather than return an unbounded list in a single response.
- **Finding**: Collections the result returns: specs, tags-union, session-notices and session-unreadable (moved to the list file past [artifacts] read-size-bytes, counts inline), and config.disabled-rule-files and config.unknown-keys (Vec<String> from the config file) which stay inline in both forms, plus session-target.scenario-detail.context-summary, the first line of a scenario's Context, whose length is unbounded. bounded_lists::place measures the whole result but only the four lists move, so a config file or scenario line large enough keeps the result past the bound. Low confidence: these grow with one operator-written file or line, not with the corpus, and the local stdio runtime is not a served API. Predates the window (b1d319f1 touched only comments here).
- **Auto-fixable**: no
- **Suggested fix**: Move config.disabled-rule-files and config.unknown-keys into the lists written by place (with counts inline), and cut context-summary to a named maximum.

## Waived findings

*None.*

## Deferred findings

### DEFERRED: QUAL-CLAIM-001 — plan-consistency reports clean over a plan.md it could not read (predates the window)

- **File**: `runtime/src/primitives/check_artifacts.rs:497-511`
- **Rule**: A result that reports a clean, empty, or in-sync state SHOULD distinguish *examined the subject and found nothing* from *could not examine the subject*. When a code path skips part of its subject, cannot reach it, or has no basis to inspect it, its output SHOULD say so — a distinct return variant, an accompanying status or guidance field, or a message naming what was not examined — not a bare zero, empty collection, or success string.
- **Finding**: check_plan_consistency returns silently when read_text(plan.md) fails (invalid UTF-8, permissions), with no finding and no skipped entry under its own family, so a spec whose plan the family never examined reads as examined-and-clean there. Driven on a planned fixture whose plan.md holds bytes 0xff 0xfe 0xfd: findings [] and clean true; the only trace is a link-adjacent-drift artifact-unreadable skip (blocking at done only through that other family). Recorded high confidence because that fixture reproduced it; a missing plan stays a legitimate silent return, and the unreadable state shows only under another family, which is the defect.
- **Auto-fixable**: no
- **Suggested fix**: Give check_plan_consistency the skipped vector and call record_unreadable_artifact(findings, skipped, "plan-consistency", status, rel path) when plan.md exists but read_text fails; keep the silent return only for a missing file.
- **Deferred**: to `specs/067-analyze-and-audit-checks/tasks.md` task 31
